<section id="pms">
<div class="container expanded row collapse">
<div class="pms-content small-12 medium-6 columns">
<h2>
                            Property Management System                        </h2>
<h3>
                            All the Tools You Need to Manage Your Property                        </h3>
<p>
</p><p>Sirvoy is a hotel reservation system and property management system that works for all types of accommodation: hotels, motels, hostels, B&amp;Bs, lodges &amp; guest houses. Your bookings are easily accessible anytime and anywhere. No installations or updates needed.</p>
<p></p>
<p>
</p><p>We aim to be the most cost-effective and user-friendly hotel reservation system on the market today, starting from only $29/month.</p>
<p></p>
<a class="pms-btn column self-align-center" href="https://sirvoy.com/features-pricing/">See All Features</a>

<div class="content small-12 medium-6 columns">
<h2 class="large-12 columns">Booking Engine</h2>
<h3>Non-Commission Direct Bookings from Your Website</h3>
<p></p><p>Start accepting bookings through your website within minutes. Sirvoy’s responsive hotel booking engine makes it easy for your guests to book online, using any type of device. Bookings are completely commission-free; it’s your own website afterall! Just add the code snippet to your website and start receiving bookings.</p>
<p></p>

<h2>
                            Channel Manager                        </h2>
<h3>
                            Direct Connections to All Major Booking Channels &amp; Travel Websites                        </h3>
<p></p><p>Today, most hotel bookings are made through online booking channels (OTAs). With Sirvoy you can easily reach millions of potential guests. Say goodbye to manual updates. Sirvoy saves you time with two-way integrations with all the important booking sites and automatically keeps your availability and bookings up-to-date.</p>
<p></p>

<script>
    function getUrlParam(name) {
        var params = window.location.search.substring(1).split('&');
        for (var i = 0; i < params.length; i++) {
            var item = params[i].split('=');
            if (item[0] == name) {
                // return value is in item[1], but we need to uri-decode first to avoid double encoding.
                // First decode with decodeURIComponent, and then replace + with spaces by regex.
                return decodeURIComponent(item[1].replace(/\+/g, ' '));
            }
        }
        return null;
    }



    // source : https://www.quirksmode.org/js/cookies.html
    function createCookie(name,value,days) {
        if (days) {
            var date = new Date();
            date.setTime(date.getTime()+(days*24*60*60*1000));
            var expires = "; expires="+date.toGMTString();
        }
        else var expires = "";
        document.cookie = name+"="+value+expires+"; path=/";
    }

    function readCookie(name) {
        var nameEQ = name + "=";
        var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
        for(var i=0;i < ca.length;i++) {
            var c = ca[i];
            while (c.charAt(0)==' ') c = c.substring(1,c.length);
            if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) == 0) return c.substring(nameEQ.length,c.length);
        }
        return null;
    }

    function eraseCookie(name) {
        createCookie(name,"",-1);
    }

    // save source
    if (document.cookie.indexOf('sirvoy_source') < 0) {
        var source_val = "";
        // google?
        if (document.referrer.indexOf('google') > -1)
            source_val = "google";
        // source param set?
        if (getUrlParam('_s') != null)
            source_val = getUrlParam('_s');
        if (source_val != "") {
            createCookie('sirvoy_source', source_val, 7)
        }
    }

    /*
    * Starting the code to set the Cookie Consent bar
    * First part is initiated from the body. On finishing loading the body, the bar will display
    * Code below is then triggered.
     */

    //Removing the Cookie Consent bar "show" class to avoid the bar being displayed if the accept Cookies cookie is placed
    function initiateCookieBar()
    {
        var cookiebar = document.getElementById("cookiebar");
        var acceptCookies=readCookie("cookieAccepted");

        if (acceptCookies=="true") {
            cookiebar.classList.remove("show");
        } else {
            cookiebar.classList.add("show");
        }
    }

    // fade out the cookiebar when pressing the OK button
    function fadeOut(el){
        el.style.opacity = 1;
        (function fade() {
            if ((el.style.opacity -= .1) < 0) {
                el.style.display = 'none';
                el.classList.add('is-hidden');
            } else {
                requestAnimationFrame(fade);
            }
        })();
    }
    var btn = document.querySelector('.js-btn');
    var el = document.querySelector('.js-fade');

    btn.addEventListener('click', function(e){
        fadeOut(el);
        createCookie("cookieAccepted", "true", 1500);
    });
    //End of cookiebar

    /*
     * Will format all the .sirvoy-timestamp spans to use local format
     */
    function refreshSirvoyTimestamps() {
        // user's timezone - we need this to format
        var tZone = Intl.DateTimeFormat().resolvedOptions().timeZone;
        if (!tZone) {
            return
	}

        // user's local - we need this to format
        var lang;
        if (window.navigator.languages) {
            lang = window.navigator.languages[0];
        } else {
            lang = window.navigator.userLanguage || window.navigator.language;
        }
        if (!lang) {
            return
	}

        var elementsToUpdate = document.getElementsByClassName("sirvoy-timestamp");
        // set locale date for each element with class "sirvoy-timestamp"
        for (var key in elementsToUpdate) {
            // check if 'key' is a number, to iterate only valid values
            var isNum = /^\d+$/.test(key);
            if (!isNum) {
                continue;
            }
		
            var timestampUtc = elementsToUpdate[key].getAttribute("data-timestamp-utc");
            var validTimestampUtc = Date.parse(timestampUtc);

            if (isNaN(validTimestampUtc) == false) {
                var date = new Date(timestampUtc);
            } else {
                continue;
            }

            var localDate = date.toLocaleString(lang, {
                timeZone: tZone,
                timeZoneName: 'short',
                year: 'numeric',
                month: 'long',
                day: 'numeric',
                hour: 'numeric',
                minute: 'numeric'
            });
		
            // update to local representation of the date
            elementsToUpdate[key].innerHTML = localDate;
        }
    }

    jQuery(document).ready(function ($){
        // update sirvoy-timestamp spans
        refreshSirvoyTimestamps();

        //first of all make sure foundation is prepared
        $(document).foundation();
        var previousScroll = 0, // previous scroll position
            menuOffset = 54, // height of menu (once scroll passed it, menu is hidden)
            detachPoint = 370, // point of detach (after scroll passed it, menu is fixed)
            hideShowOffset = 6; // scrolling value after which triggers hide/show menu
        // on scroll hide/show menu
        $('#mobilemenu').mmenu();
        $(window).scroll(function() {
            var currentScroll = $(this).scrollTop(), // gets current scroll position
                scrollDifference = Math.abs(currentScroll - previousScroll); // calculates how fast user is scrolling
            // if scrolled past menu
            if (currentScroll > menuOffset) {
                // if scrolled past detach point add class to fix menu
                if (currentScroll > detachPoint) {
                    if (!$('header').hasClass('detached'))
                        $('header').addClass('detached');
                }
                // if scrolling faster than hideShowOffset hide/show menu
                if (scrollDifference >= hideShowOffset) {
                    if (currentScroll > previousScroll) {
                        // scrolling down; hide menu
                        if (!$('header').hasClass('noshow'))
                            $('header').addClass('noshow');
                    } else {
                        // scrolling up; show menu
                        if ($('header').hasClass('noshow'))
                            $('header').removeClass('noshow');
                    }
                }
            } else {
                // only remove “detached” class if user is at the top of document (menu jump fix)
                if (currentScroll <= 0){
                    $('header').removeClass();
                }
            }
            // if user is at the bottom of document show menu
            if ((window.innerHeight + window.scrollY) >= document.body.offsetHeight) {
                $('header').removeClass('invisible');
            }
            // replace previous scroll position with new one
            previousScroll = currentScroll;
        })
        // Some small countries dosn't exist in this map so check if any JS error and remove them from array if so.
        //do not try this if element map-container dont exist
        if(document.getElementById('map-container')) {

            var bubble_map = new Datamap({
                element: document.getElementById('map-container'),
                responsive: true,
                projection: 'mercator',
                fills: {
                    defaultFill: "rgba(67,69,139,.1)",
                    border: '#000000',
                    SIRVOY: '#F05724'
                },
                geographyConfig: {
                    borderWidth: 1,
                    borderOpacity: 1,
                    borderColor: '#dae1eb',
                    highlightOnHover: false,
                    popupOnHover: false
                },

                bubblesConfig: {
                    borderWidth: 0,
                    borderOpacity: 1,
                    borderColor: '#eef4f7',
                    popupOnHover: false,
                    highlightOnHover: false,

        var starterPrices = ["19","24","29","39","49","59"];
        var proPrices = ["45","69","89","109","129","149"];
        $('[data-slider]').on('moved.zf.slider', function(){
            // check which tier based on room count
            var rooms = $('#room-counter').val();
            var tier = 5;
            if (rooms < 101) tier = 4;
            if (rooms < 51) tier = 3;
            if (rooms < 21) tier = 2;
            if (rooms < 11) tier = 1;
            if (rooms < 6) tier = 0;

            $('#starter-price').html(starterPrices[tier]);
            $('#pro-price').html(proPrices[tier]);

            $('#prices .text-room-counter').html((rooms != 101) ? $('#room-counter').val() : '100+');
        });

        if (bubble_map) {
            // resize the map when resizing window
            window.onresize = function () {
                bubble_map.resize();
                var divHeight = $('#map-container svg').height();
                $('#section-map').css('height', divHeight+'px');
            }
            var divHeight = $('#map-container svg').height();
            $('#section-map').css('height', divHeight+'px');
        }
    });

</script>
